### 3.8 Jobs, templates and job-like loops, 0.4

The 3 ready-made templates are `JOB4-01` to `JOB4-03`. The other rows are presets, job-model features, and background loops in the hub that behave like scheduled jobs. Loops already listed in 3.5 or 3.7 are not repeated here.

| ID | What it is | Where | What it does | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| JOB4-01 | Template `security-scan` | 0.4: `rook/hub/plugins/jobs/catalog.py`; cap `routine.security_scan` in `rook/hub/plugins/routines/__init__.py`, `security.py` | Every 5 minutes: open workers, console rooms, MCP identities and dashboard sign-ins, plus denied calls, refused keys, new keys and access changes, compared with the last scan. Quiet unless something changed; urgent on a burst of refused keys. Needs no config. | Not covered |
| JOB4-02 | Template `attention-digest` | 0.4: `catalog.py`; `routine.attention_digest` (`routines/digest.py`) | Every 2 hours from 08:00 to 20:00: one quiet message of what came in from the phones' feeds, chat rooms (Telegram and Discord included) and, optionally, IMAP or a mail cap. | Not covered (draws on chat bridges, which are Agents) |
| JOB4-03 | Template `camera-review` | 0.4: `catalog.py`; `routine.camera_review` (`routines/cameras.py`) | Every 10 minutes: a frame per camera (snapshot URL, RTSP through ffmpeg on a worker, or `camera.snap`), a cheap change test on the hub, and a vision model only for frames that changed. | Not covered |
| JOB4-04 | Routine caps run directly, with `dry_run` | 0.4: `routines/__init__.py` (owners only, scope multi-user) | The three caps work without a job; `dry_run` shows the report without saving or sending. | Not covered |
| JOB4-05 | Editor's new-job skeleton | 0.4: `rook/web/jobs.js` `TEMPLATE` | The starting JSON for "+ New". | Not covered |
| JOB4-06 | Claude Code ready-made rules (3) | 0.4: `rook/web/ccmod.js` `EXAMPLES` | "Tell me when a session is waiting", "Never force-delete folders", "Update the Rook task before stopping". | Agents or Workbench |
| JOB4-07 | Role presets (25, plus categories) | 0.4: `rook/hub/plugins/roles/presets/*.toml` (analyst, architect, cad, comms, community, content, editor, fab, firmware, household, launch, lead, ml, pcb, pm, product, qa, release, research, security, skeptic, social, sre, style, writer; `categories.toml`) | Starting roles for huddles. | Agents or Workbench |
| JOB4-08 | Plugin presets (6) | 0.4: `rook/hub/plugin_profiles.py` `PRESETS`: remote, agents, knowledge, home, alerts, hardware | "What do you want Rook to do?" groups; band defaults for new workers. | Not covered |
| JOB4-09 | Start page default layout | 0.4: `rook/hub/plugins/ui/layout.py` `default()` | Workers, Open tasks, Chat rooms, Recently changed pages, Getting around, Make it yours. | Not covered |
| JOB4-10 | Huddle standup pattern | 0.4: `rook/hub/plugins/jobs/huddle_kind.py` docstring | A cron job with one `huddle` step ("What's on deck today?") delivered to Telegram, voice or notify. | Agents or Workbench |
| JOB4-11 | A job is a graph of steps | 0.4: `jobs/model.py` (`TOP_KEYS`, `entry`, `steps`, `vars`), `docs/design/jobs.md` §4-5 | A named job with triggers, an `entry` step, a dict of steps that branch to each other, `vars`, and a description. Up to 200 steps. | Out by decision: J6 "No workflow DSL in the hub." *(plan line, not in Explicitly-out list)*; see section 2 |
| JOB4-12 | Step `cap` | 0.4: `jobs/kinds.py` `run_cap` | Calls one cap on a worker (by name, id or filter; retries while it is offline). Denied counts as blocked; `ok:false` as failure. | Covered: J1 |
| JOB4-13 | Step `fanout` | 0.4: `kinds.py` `run_fanout` | Runs the same cap on every worker matching a filter (`has_cap`, `os`, `names`, `tags`), with a join rule over the results. | Out by decision: J6 *(plan line)* |
| JOB4-14 | Step `tool` | 0.4: `kinds.py` `run_tool`; `jobs/runtime.py` | Calls a hub MCP tool (for example `rook_task`) as the run's identity. | Out by decision: J6 *(plan line)* |
| JOB4-15 | Step `wait` | 0.4: `kinds.py` `run_wait` | Waits a duration. | Out by decision: J6 *(plan line)* |
| JOB4-16 | Step `notify` | 0.4: `kinds.py` `notify_call`, `VIAS` | Sends a message by voice (`voice.speak`), phone notification (`notify.post`) or Telegram (`notify.send`). | Out by decision: J6 *(plan line)* |
| JOB4-17 | Step `ask` | 0.4: `jobs/agent_kinds.py` `run_ask` | Speaks a question on a phone and waits for the reply; the reply text is saved in the run; no answer means failure. | Agents or Workbench |
| JOB4-18 | Step `agent` | 0.4: `agent_kinds.py` `run_agent`; `rook/hub/plugins/home/job_agent.py` | Hands a prompt to the house agent, a role, or a Claude or Codex session on a worker. Modes: delegate or wait for a verdict. Has a tools scope, model, max tool calls and a budget. The agent's `finish(complete=true)` can end the job. | Agents or Workbench |
| JOB4-19 | Step `huddle` | 0.4: `jobs/huddle_kind.py` | Runs one round of a chaired huddle and delivers the summary. | Agents or Workbench |
| JOB4-20 | Step `join` and its expressions | 0.4: `kinds.py` `run_join`; `jobs/expr.py` | Waits on branches: all, any, at least N, or an expression such as `steps.a.ok and not steps.b.ok`, parsed safely with `ast`. Fires once per run. | Out by decision: J6 *(plan line)* |
| JOB4-21 | Step `noop` | 0.4: `kinds.py` | Does nothing; useful as a branch point. | Out by decision: J6 *(plan line)* |
| JOB4-22 | Branching, parallel branches, loops | 0.4: `jobs/executor.py` | Next steps on success, failure or hang, run in parallel. Loops back are allowed, capped by `max_step_executions` (100). | Out by decision: J6 *(plan line)* |
| JOB4-23 | Step timeout, retry and success rules | 0.4: `executor.py`, `jobs/steps.py` `apply_success_rules` | Default 5-minute timeout (a timeout is `hang`), `retry {max, delay}`, success by exit codes or `match`/`not_match`, `allow_failure`. | Not covered |
| JOB4-24 | Run states | 0.4: `executor.py`; `jobs.js` `FINAL` | success, failure, hang, interrupted, dropped, blocked, cancelled. A run takes its worst step's state. | Not covered (J3 says only success or fail) |
| JOB4-25 | Trigger `cron` | 0.4: `jobs/cron.py`, `model.py` `_triggers` | Five-field cron plus `@hourly`, `@daily` and the like. Per-trigger time zone; DST rules (a skipped time fires once at the jump; a repeated time fires on the first pass). | Covered: J2 (schedule) |
| JOB4-26 | Trigger `at` | 0.4: `cron.py` `parse_instant` | Fires once at a set instant; warns if it is in the past. | Covered: J2 (schedule) |
| JOB4-27 | Trigger `after` | 0.4: `model.py`, `jobs/scheduler.py` `arm_after` | Re-runs the job a set time after its own last run finished (on success, finish or failure). | Not covered (J3 chains on other jobs' completion events; 0.4's `after` is the same job only) |
| JOB4-28 | Trigger `manual` and Run now | 0.4: `model.py` default trigger; `service.py` `run` | A job with no schedule; started by hand or by an agent. | Not covered |
| JOB4-29 | Overlap modes | 0.4: `model.py` `OVERLAP_MODES` | When a trigger fires during a run: queue (optional `max_queue`), skip, or run in parallel. | Not covered |
| JOB4-30 | Missed runs | 0.4: `model.py` `MISSED_MODES`; scheduler | After hub downtime: run once, skip, or run all, within a 10-minute grace; such runs are marked `missed`. | Not covered |
| JOB4-31 | Run-history retention and pruning | 0.4: `jobs/store.py` `prune`; setting `job.retention_days` (30); per-job `retention_days` | Deletes finished runs past retention. | Out by decision: "Stream timeouts, blob garbage collection, any automatic deletion" |
| JOB4-32 | Delete job | 0.4: `store.py` `delete_job` | Hard-deletes the job, its triggers, history, notices and finished runs; asks a running run to cancel. | Covered: J7 (differs: runs and history are deleted too) |
| JOB4-33 | Enable and disable | 0.4: `service.py` | Turns a job on or off. | Covered: J7 (pause) |
| JOB4-34 | Access rules | 0.4: `model.py` `check_access`; `service.py`; setting `job.default_access` | Principal patterns decide who may read, edit and run a job. The owner and the operator always may. | Out by decision: "Multi-user tenants and permissions beyond share cap lists" |
| JOB4-35 | Identity modes | 0.4: `jobs/identity.py`, `jobs/principals.py` | A job runs as its creator, a named key or user, a key kept in the vault, or a fallback. The role is rechecked every run. A revoked identity blocks the run and pauses the job; a removed person pauses their jobs. | Not covered (T5 ownership chain is related) |
| JOB4-36 | Run-as-both for manual runs | 0.4: `service.py` (`run_by`) | When someone runs another person's job, each call is checked against both the job's identity and the caller. | Out by decision: "Multi-user tenants and permissions beyond share cap lists" |
| JOB4-37 | Takeover on edit | 0.4: `service.py` | Editing a job someone else owns makes the editor its owner and resets its identity to creator. History records it. | Not covered |
| JOB4-38 | Guardrails | 0.4: `jobs/guardrails.py`; setting `job.guardrails`; `guardrails_preview`, `set_guardrails` | Default deny list for job steps (admin tier, deauth, enrollment, updates, restarts, policy and settings changes, destructive deletes); per-job deny; operator-only allow; inherit; a preview of what a change would block. A blocked step is never retried. | Out by decision: "Approvals, security gates, elevation leases, change classes" |
| JOB4-39 | Locks | 0.4: `service.py` `lock`/`unlock` (`docs/design/jobs.md` §7.1) | Only a person can lock or unlock a job. A locked job cannot be changed, switched off or deleted by any agent or token. | Out by decision: "Approvals, security gates, elevation leases, change classes" |
| JOB4-40 | Jobs that end themselves | 0.4: `model.py` `LIMIT_KEYS`; `scheduler.py`; setting `job.max_failures` | `max_runs`, `until` (an expiry), `max_failures` in a row (with a hub default), `complete_on`; a step's `complete`; an agent's `finish(complete)`. The job switches off with a recorded reason. | Out by decision: Build rules "No gates, limits, expiries, approvals or review processes until core is stable." *(plan line, not in Explicitly-out list)* |
| JOB4-41 | Repeated-message dedupe | 0.4: `jobs/notices.py`; `limits.notify_window`, setting `job.notify_window` | The same text to the same target is sent at most once per window. Text is compared loosely (numbers ignored); only a digest is stored. | Not covered (a limit; see the Build rules) |
| JOB4-42 | Secrets in step arguments | 0.4: `runtime.py`; `executor.py` | `{{secret:name}}` resolves at the moment of the call. Output is masked and truncated when stored, and masked again on read. Prompts may not contain secrets. | Covered: X2, X3, L4 |
| JOB4-43 | Job history rows | 0.4: `store.py` `add_history` | A per-job log: create, update, lock, unlock, guardrail_blocked, takeover. | Covered: L1, J8 (differs: a separate table, deleted with the job) |
| JOB4-44 | Revisions | 0.4: `service.py` (`expect_revision`) | Optimistic concurrency on update. | Not covered |
| JOB4-45 | Leases for several processes | 0.4: `store.py`; `scheduler.py` | A run holds a lease. Runs whose lease runs out become `interrupted`. Trigger fires and run claims are atomic, so several hub processes can share the database. | Out by decision: F3 "Failover and whole-hub federation are LATER." |
| JOB4-46 | Job settings | 0.4: `jobs/__init__.py` `SETTINGS` | `enabled`, `timezone` (America/Toronto by default), `retention_days`, `max_step_executions`, `notify_worker`, `default_agent`, `max_failures`, `notify_window`, `tick_seconds`, `db_path`, `guardrails`, `default_access`, `default_fallback`. | Not covered |
| JOB4-47 | MCP and caps for jobs | 0.4: `jobs/service.py`; `rook/hub/mcp_tools.py` (`rook_jobs`); caps `job.read`, `job.write` on worker `rook` | Reads: list, get, runs, run_get, next, validate, guardrails_preview, describe_schema, templates. Writes: create, update, delete, enable, disable, run, cancel, set_guardrails, settings, lock, unlock, install. | Covered: J7 (differs: a dedicated MCP tool, see section 2) |
| JOB4-48 | Dashboard and terminal access to jobs | 0.4: `jobs/web.py` (`/jobs/account-api`); `rook/remote/jobs_web.py` (`/api/band/jobs`); `rook/cli/band_tui.py` (`J` key) | The Jobs page API; the `rook band` panel lists jobs, shows runs, runs, enables or disables, shows JSON. | Not covered (the TUI) |
| JOB4-49 | Skills learned from job steps | 0.4: `rook/hub/plugins/memory` | The house agent keeps a way of doing something that worked in a job step; it is used only after you approve it. | Agents or Workbench |
| JOB4-50 | Jobs scheduler loop | 0.4: `jobs/scheduler.py` `run_forever`, `tick` (every 5 s) | Interrupts expired leases, arms `after` triggers, fires due triggers, promotes queued runs, claims and starts runs, prunes history. | Covered: J7 (jobs run at the hub) |
| JOB4-51 | Attention alert expiry | 0.4: `attention/service.py` `_expire_alerts` | Marks alerts expired after a time limit (`ALERT_TTL`) and keeps only the last N. | Out by decision: "any automatic deletion" |
| JOB4-52 | Change management loop | 0.4: `rook/hub/plugins/changes/__init__.py` `_loop`, `tick`, `_digest_due`; `changes/runner.py` | Runs change requests and sends a daily or weekly change digest. | Out by decision: "Approvals, security gates, elevation leases, change classes" |
| JOB4-53 | Memory consolidation and retention | 0.4: `rook/hub/plugins/memory/__init__.py` `_tidy_loop` (`consolidate_minutes`, 30); `memory.retention_days` (90) | Summarises and rolls up conversations, merges duplicates, fades unused memories; deletes replaced and forgotten ones after 90 days. | Agents or Workbench (the deletion also meets "any automatic deletion") |

| JOB4-56 | Settings store watch | 0.4: `rook/hub/plugins/settings.py` `_watch_store` | Applies settings saved by another hub process. | Not covered |
| JOB4-57 | Roster eviction | 0.4: `rook/band_mcp/client.py` `_gc_loop` (every 15 s, `WORKER_STALE_SECS`=90) | Removes workers silent for 90 s from the live roster, along with their devices. | Covered: H3 (differs: the plan keeps the record and caps) |
| JOB4-58 | Terminal stream sweep | 0.4: `rook/remote/term_hub.py` `_sweep_loop` (every 30 s) | Drops terminal streams with no viewers once they have been idle too long. | Out by decision: "Stream timeouts, blob garbage collection, any automatic deletion" |
| JOB4-59 | Approvals hold wait | 0.4: `rook/hub/approvals.py` (wait loop) | A held call waits for a decision. | Out by decision: "Approvals, security gates…" |

### 3.9 Clients, firmware, installers, skill and conformance, 0.4

| ID | What it is | Where | What it does | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| APP4-001 | Worker foreground service | 0.4: `A/WorkerService.kt`; manifest `.WorkerService` (FGS types dataSync, mediaProjection, location) | Runs the embedded Python band worker on its own thread for the life of the app, with a persistent notification, partial wake lock and high-performance Wi-Fi lock. | Covered: U2, W2 (U2 "supervises"; the Kotlin-host-plus-Python-worker shape differs, see differs list) |
| APP4-002 | Boot and update autostart | 0.4: `A/BootReceiver.kt`; manifest receiver for BOOT_COMPLETED, LOCKED_BOOT_COMPLETED, MY_PACKAGE_REPLACED, SCHEDULE_EXACT_ALARM_PERMISSION_STATE_CHANGED | Restarts the worker after a reboot or an APK replacement (only if the person started it once, `autostart` pref), re-arms voice timers, finishes the APK update record. | Covered: U2 (supervision; boot autostart itself is not spelled out) |
| APP4-003 | Embedded worker entry and plugin host | 0.4: `AP/worker_entry.py` | Boots the unmodified `rook.worker` inside Chaquopy, adds `rook_android.plugins` to the plugin host, reports the fact `app="android"` so native plugins replace the stock screenshot/hid/battery/selfupdate modules, logs to logcat and a file. | Covered: PL3, PL7 (0.4 bundles worker parts in the APK; see differs) |
| APP4-004 | Worker lifecycle loop | 0.4: `AP/rook_android/worker_runtime.py` | Reconnects in-process with jittered backoff (2 to 60 s), rebuilds the link after 120 s off the band, re-reads hub/PSK/name every 30 s and restarts the worker when they change, leaves the band if device authorization is refused. | Not covered (plan Layering gives transport to Telesthete; no plan line on client reconnect policy) |
| APP4-005 | Network-change reconnect | 0.4: `A/WorkerService.kt` (default-network callback) + `worker_runtime.kick()` | On a new default network, drops the band link and reconnects at once on the new network. | Not covered |
| APP4-006 | Device-config refresh with device certificate | 0.4: `worker_runtime.desired()` calling `rook.worker.enroll.refresh` | For an account-enrolled phone, proves the device key to the server every 30 s, fetches the current band (hub, PSK, epoch) and applies it; falls back to typed hub/PSK when not enrolled. Also applies `wconfig` pushed config (`worker.config_apply`, `boot_reconcile`). | Covered: CF2 (mechanism differs, see differs) |
| APP4-007 | App version on announce | 0.4: `worker_runtime.py` (`worker.app_release`) | Puts the APK version name and code into the worker's announce and `worker.status`. | Covered: H1, U6 |

| APP4-010 | Pairing-code enrollment on the phone | 0.4: `A/GoogleEnrollment.pair` | Six-character pairing code POSTed to `/enroll`; returns one band's hub and PSK. | Not covered (A2 says "No code entry"; see differs) |

### 3.10 Site pages and page features, 0.5

The 11 pages are `PAGE5-01` to `PAGE5-11`: 3 static (shell, sign-in, account) and 8 from manifests. The remaining rows are page features and components.

| ID | What it is | Where | What it does | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| PAGE5-01 | Shell (site frame + nav) | 0.5: `rook/core2/hub/doors/web/static/shell.html`, `shell.js`, `shell.css`; `GET /` | Signed-in frame: sidebar by section, More, Archived+Restore, title bar (About / Archive / Chat), sign-out, phone menu button; opens pages in a sandboxed iframe | Covered: P1, P2 |
| PAGE5-02 | Sign-in page | 0.5: `static/login.html`, `login.js`; `GET/POST /login` | User name + password form. JSON or form post. Also accepts `{token}` for a person's own door token (desktop app); an agent token is refused | Covered: T4 |
| PAGE5-03 | Account page | 0.5: `static/account.html`, `account.js`; `GET /account` | Change your own password (current/new/again), first-run notice for a generated password, and the MCP door address for a claude.ai custom connector | Not covered |
| PAGE5-04 | Workers | 0.5: `rook/core2/hub/doors/web/builtin/hub/pages/workers.html` (`hub/workers`, nav Hub) | Table: online dot, name, problem "!", plugin count, last seen. Click a row for id, core, version, about, plugins, facts, "can't serve X" reasons, problem | Covered: P4 |
| PAGE5-05 | Tokens | 0.5: `builtin/token/pages/tokens.html` (`token/tokens`, nav Hub) | "New token": agent name + label → token shown once. Table: Agent / For / Label / Created, with Revoke | Covered: P4, T6 |
| PAGE5-06 | Journal | 0.5: `builtin/journal/pages/journal.html` (`journal/journal`, no nav → More) | Filters: cap, worker, kind (call / session / late_reply). Table: When / Who / Call / Why / Outcome, with "More" paging | Covered: P4, L2 |
| PAGE5-07 | Settings | 0.5: `builtin/setting/pages/settings.html` (`setting/settings`, no nav → More) | Hub block (hub id, band, core, doors, security on/off). People table for owners (name, preset, extra grants; read-only). Plugin-settings editor (typed inputs, Save, Back to default, source set/config/default). Plugins table (title, version, built in, runs on, cap count, pages) | Covered: P4 |
| PAGE5-08 | Approvals | 0.5: `builtin/approval/pages/approvals.html` (`approval/approvals`, nav Hub) | Held calls (cap, worker, why, who, reasons, args) with Approve/Refuse; agents' questions (`approval.ask`) answered with option buttons or free text | Out by decision: "Approvals, security gates, elevation leases, change classes" |
| PAGE5-09 | Knowledge | 0.5: `rook/plugins/knowledge/pages/knowledge.html` (nav Work) | Search the wiki, list entries, open one | Agents or Workbench |
| PAGE5-10 | Projects | 0.5: `rook/plugins/projects/pages/projects.html` (nav Work) | Table: project, state, open tasks, changed; click opens a page | Agents or Workbench |

Default section "Pages". Each change is added to NOTE.md's history | Not covered |
| PAGE5-13 | Sandboxed page frames | 0.5: `site.py PAGE_CSP`, `_open`, `_frame`; `GET /web/frame/<nonce>` | Each page is its own opaque origin (CSP `sandbox allow-scripts`, `connect-src 'none'`). One-time frame URL (60 s), 12 h page token, at most 32 tokens per session. The shell closes a frame that navigates away | Not covered |
| PAGE5-14 | Page bridge (`window.rook`) | 0.5: `static/bridge.js`, injected by `site.py inject()` | `rook.call(cap,args,{why,worker})`, `rook.on(pattern,fn)`, `rook.open(page)`, `rook.component(fn)`, `esc/ago/time`, all over postMessage to the shell | Covered: P1 |
| PAGE5-15 | Page-declared caps and events as a rights layer | 0.5: `site.py _page_call` (`["page",{grants: page.caps}]` + author layers), `_poll` | A page can only call the caps it declares and hear the events it declares, and only as far as the viewer may | Out by decision: "Multi-user tenants and permissions beyond share cap lists" |
| PAGE5-16 | Live events to pages | 0.5: `site.py _on_event`, `_whose`, `_poll`; `GET /web/api/events` | Hub events reach the open page by 20 s long poll (last 500 kept), filtered by the event's person (owners see all) and the page's declared events. Tainting events taint the page's dispatch session | Not covered |
| PAGE5-17 | Agent-made page acts as its author | 0.5: `site.py _page_caller`, `_authors` | Calls from an agent-made page run as its first agent author on the viewer's behalf, never with more than any author may; such a page cannot call `approval.decide` | Out by decision: "Multi-user tenants and permissions beyond share cap lists" |
| PAGE5-18 | "About" provenance panel | 0.5: `shell.js #note-btn` → `page.get` | Shows "Made by <agent> for <person>" and NOTE.md, or "From a plugin <name> <version> (built in)", plus "May call" and "Hears" | Not covered |
| PAGE5-19 | Archive / restore pages | 0.5: caps `page.archive/restore`; `<data>/pages.json` | Takes any page (plugin or agent-made) off the site, reversibly; Archived list in the sidebar | Not covered |
| PAGE5-20 | Off-sidebar pages ("More") | 0.5: `caps.py Page.nav`; `shell.js loadNav` | A manifest page with no `nav` goes under More but still opens by its address | Not covered |
| PAGE5-21 | Start page | 0.5: caps `page.start`, `page.site`; `pages.json` `start` | Each person can have their own start page. An agent or job acting alone sets the hub-wide one (`*`). Fallback is `hub/workers` | Not covered |
| PAGE5-22 | Layout pages from components | 0.5: `caps.py Pages.arrange`, cap `page.components` | A page made of up to 50 `{component, props, title?, wide?}` cards; it may call the union of its components' caps | Not covered |
| PAGE5-23 | Page built under a workbench task | 0.5: `caps.py _built_under`, `_task_call` (`task.link relation=produced`) | With the workbench installed, every agent-made page is linked to a task (one is made if none is given) | Agents or Workbench |
| PAGE5-24 | House-agent chat dock | 0.5: `shell.html #dock`, `shell.js startDock/say/read`; setting `page.dock` (default `house`); `site.py DOCK_CAPS` | Chat beside every page. Each message is prefixed with "[site] The person is on the page X". On first visit it prompts the agent to welcome the person and offer pages and a start page. Open state kept in localStorage. Refreshes nav after the agent's turn | Agents or Workbench |
| PAGE5-25 | Shell limited to `page.*` | 0.5: `site.py /web/api/call` | The shell's own calls may only be `page.*` (plus the dock agent's chat); page calls go through `/web/api/page` with the page token | Not covered |
| PAGE5-26 | Hold answers only from shipped pages, "present" | 0.5: `site.py _decide` | `approval.decide` from a shipped page counts the person as present, so they may answer hard stops; the cap alone never may | Out by decision: "Approvals, security gates, elevation leases, change classes" |

### 3.11 Jobs, templates and hooks, 0.5

The 4 manifest job templates are `JOB5-01` to `JOB5-04`. The other rows are job-model features and plugin hooks.

| ID | What it is | Where | What it does | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| JOB5-01 | `job/security-scan` | 0.5: `rook/plugins/jobs/plugin.yaml` `jobs:` + `templates/security_scan.py` | Every 5 min: counts denied and held calls in the journal since the last run; notifies the owners when denials reach `vars.threshold` (default 10) | Out by decision: "Approvals, security gates, elevation leases, change classes" |
| JOB5-02 | `backup/nightly` | 0.5: `rook/core2/hub/backup/plugin.yaml` `jobs:` | `cron: "40 3 * * *"` (jobs timezone), `call: backup.run`, `catch_up: true` | Covered: F3, J1, J2 |
| JOB5-03 | `attention/attention` | 0.5: `rook/plugins/attention/plugin.yaml` `jobs:` | Every 15 min the house agent (persona `house`) runs its attention skill over what arrived for people | Agents or Workbench |
| JOB5-04 | `security-ml/retrain` | 0.5: `rook/plugins/security-ml/plugin.yaml` `jobs:` | `cron: "17 3 * * *"`, `call: security-ml.train`, `as: creator` | Out by decision: "Approvals, security gates, elevation leases, change classes" |
| JOB5-05 | Migrated 0.4 jobs | 0.5: `castling/switchover/migrate/records.py` (repo tooling, not shipped) | Each 0.4 job becomes a disabled, manual-only 0.5 job of the same name, owned by the hub owner, with the 0.4 definition in `vars.definition04` | Not covered |
| JOB5-06 | Job = record | 0.5: `jobs/plugin.yaml records.job` | Fields: name (pattern), doc, trigger, call/worker/args, agent, script, plugin/run, run_as, persona, vars, enabled, retries (0-10), catch_up, timeout, notify, source, source_version, edited; `history: true`; `private: true` | Covered: J1 |
| JOB5-07 | Exactly one action per job | 0.5: `scripts/jobs.py KINDS`, `check_job` | A job does exactly one of: a cap call, an agent prompt, a script, or a plugin's script (`run`) | Covered: J1 |
| JOB5-08 | Trigger `cron` | 0.5: `jobs.py Cron`, `_ALIASES`; setting `timezone` (default UTC) | Five-field cron with month/day names and `@hourly/@daily/@weekly...`, in an IANA zone | Covered: J2 |
| JOB5-09 | Trigger `every` | 0.5: `jobs.py check_trigger` | Fixed interval, 1 s or more | Covered: J2 |
| JOB5-10 | Trigger `on` event + `filter` | 0.5: `jobs.py on_event`, `_matches`; hook `on: [{event: "*"}]` | Fires on any hub event matching a glob. Filter keys are dotted payload paths; a list means any of. Never fires on `job.*` events | Covered: J1, J2 |
| JOB5-11 | Trigger `manual` + `job.run` | 0.5: `jobs.py start_run` | Start any job now as its own identity, merging `vars`; `wait` answers when the run ends (cap timeout 30 min) | Not covered |
| JOB5-12 | Trigger `webhook` with HMAC per job | 0.5: route `routes.hook /hook auth: none` → `/p/job/hook?job=<id>`; `jobs.py webhook`, `_key`; cap `job.webhook` | Signed POST: `X-Rook-Signature: sha256=<hex HMAC>`, or GitHub's `X-Hub-Signature-256`; per-job key; parsed body becomes `job.payload` | Covered: J2 |
| JOB5-13 | Call jobs | 0.5: `jobs.py perform` | One cap call with fixed `args` on `worker` (blank: whichever worker serves it) | Covered: J1 |
| JOB5-14 | Agent jobs | 0.5: `jobs.py perform`; setting `default_agent` (default `house`) | `{persona?, prompt}`: asks an agent | Agents or Workbench |
| JOB5-15 | Script jobs + workflow library | 0.5: `jobs.py script/serve`; `scripts/child.py Job` | Python `main(job)` in its own stdlib-only process and scratch folder. Library: `job.call(cap,args,on=,why=)`, `job.ask`, `job.notify`, `job.wait`, `job.retry(fn,times,wait)`, `job.parallel`, `job.log`/print; `job.vars/event/payload/last/trigger/attempt`; a failed call raises `job.Error`; the return value is the result | Not covered |
| JOB5-16 | Plugin-shipped scripts (`run: path.py:function`) | 0.5: `jobs.py plugin_script`, `template_fields` | A template's code runs from the shipping plugin's folder | Not covered |
| JOB5-17 | `job.ask` waits on a person | 0.5: `jobs.py ask`, `MAX_ASK = 3600` | The question goes out via `approval.ask` (Approvals page + house agent). The run state is `waiting`; it returns None on timeout (at most 1 h) | Not covered |
| JOB5-18 | Run as creator | 0.5: `jobs.py Runtime.identity`, `_as` | Calls run as the person (or agent-for-person) who made the job, with their rights re-checked each run | Not covered |
| JOB5-19 | Run as persona | 0.5: `run_as: persona`, `persona:` → `agent:<persona>` | Owners only: calls run as the persona agent's own grants | Agents or Workbench |
| JOB5-20 | Owner-only parts | 0.5: `jobs.py check_job(owner)` | Scripts, `on` triggers and persona jobs are owners only | Out by decision: "Multi-user tenants and permissions beyond share cap lists" |
| JOB5-21 | Retries with backoff | 0.5: `retries`; setting `retry_after` (1 m, doubling) | Re-runs a failed run (`trigger: retry`) | Not covered |
| JOB5-22 | `catch_up` | 0.5: `jobs.py schedule` | A cron/every job runs once at start if a slot was missed while the hub was down; otherwise missed slots are skipped | Not covered |
| JOB5-23 | Run timeout | 0.5: field `timeout`; setting `timeout` (10 m) | Stops a run past its limit | Not covered |
| JOB5-24 | One run per job at a time | 0.5: `jobs.py fire` | A trigger that finds the job running is skipped (logged) | Not covered |
| JOB5-25 | Notify on failure/always/never | 0.5: `notify` field; `jobs.py notify/tell` | Run outcome goes to the house agent (`default_agent`) to reach the person; without agents, to `notify.send` | Agents or Workbench |
| JOB5-26 | Runs are records | 0.5: `records.run` (job ref, name, trigger, scheduled, attempt, by, as_who, state running/waiting/ok/failed/cancelled, started, ended, result, error, log, asking, event) | One run record covers the whole flow | Covered: J5, K6 (0.5 keeps runs as records with 30-day retention; see differences) |
| JOB5-27 | Run retention 30 days | 0.5: `records.run retain: 30d`; `RecordStore.prune` daily (`runtime._daily`) | Run records older than 30 days are erased | Out by decision: "Stream timeouts, blob garbage collection, any automatic deletion" |
| JOB5-28 | Jobs and runs are private | 0.5: `private: true` on both types | Seen only by their person and owners | Out by decision: "Multi-user tenants and permissions beyond share cap lists" |
| JOB5-29 | Edit with revision; history | 0.5: `job.update(id, revision)`; `history: true` | Optimistic update; every write snapshotted; `job.history` | Not covered |
| JOB5-30 | Delete is archive | 0.5: generated `job.archive` (no `delete` verb for `job`) | Soft-delete: the row stays with `archived` set and can be restored | Covered: J7 (0.5 archives instead of deleting; see differences) |
| JOB5-31 | Cancel a run | 0.5: `job.cancel` → `jobs.py cancel`, `_kill` | Stops a running run and kills its script process | Not covered |
| JOB5-32 | Templates + install | 0.5: `job.templates`, `job.install`; `hub.manifests section=jobs` | Lists every plugin's manifest jobs with installed state; installs one as yours (optional name/vars/enabled) | Not covered |
| JOB5-33 | Auto-seeding persona templates | 0.5: `jobs.py seed()` (`SEED_EVERY = 3600`, and on worker events) | `enabled: true, as: persona` templates are installed active; `edited: true` (changed by a person) stops plugin updates overwriting them | Not covered |
| JOB5-34 | Interrupted runs fail at restart | 0.5: `jobs.py recover()` | No checkpoints: a run open when the hub stopped is marked failed ("interrupted: the hub restarted…") at the next start, then retried or reported | Not covered |
| JOB5-35 | Tainted runs | 0.5: `jobs.py fire` (`event.taints` → `Caller.tainted`) | A run started by an outside-content event or webhook makes tainted calls | Out by decision: "Approvals, security gates, elevation leases, change classes" |
| JOB5-36 | All job calls go through dispatch | 0.5: `ctx.call_as`; manual | Every job call carries a why, passes rights/gates/holds and is journaled | Covered: L1 |
| JOB5-37 | Scheduler tick | 0.5: setting `tick` (1 s) | How often due jobs are checked | Not covered |
| JOB5-38 | Hook scheduler | 0.5: `rook/kit/hooks.py`; `rook/core2/hub/plugins.py` | Runs plugin `every`/`on` hooks, held to the plugin's `uses`. Taint propagates. One hook failing doesn't stop the others | Not covered |
| JOB5-39 | `agent` every 30 m `tick` | 0.5: `rook/plugins/agents/plugin.yaml` | Agents runtime upkeep | Agents or Workbench |
| JOB5-40 | `agent` on telegram.message, discord.message, approval.asked, task.signoff_requested, agent.agent.created/updated/archived | 0.5: same | Inbound chat to the house agent, relaying questions, sign-offs, agent lifecycle | Agents or Workbench |
| JOB5-41 | `browser` every 30 s `Browser.reap` | 0.5: `rook/plugins/browser/plugin.yaml` | Closes idle browser sessions (`idle_timeout`) | Out by decision: Build rules, "No gates, limits, expiries, approvals or review processes until core is stable. Hardening is a later pass." |
| JOB5-42 | `notification-android` every 30 s `watch`, 10 s `watch_cards` | 0.5: `rook/plugins/notification-android/plugin.yaml` | Polls phone notifications and emits events | Not covered |
| JOB5-43 | `sms` every 30 s `watch` | 0.5: `rook/plugins/sms/plugin.yaml` | Polls SMS and emits `sms.received` | Not covered |
| JOB5-44 | `security` every 5 m `save`; on hold.created / hold.decided | 0.5: `rook/plugins/security/plugin.yaml` | Saves gate state; wakes the triage agent on holds | Out by decision: "Approvals, security gates, elevation leases, change classes" |
| JOB5-45 | `job` on `*` | 0.5: `rook/plugins/jobs/plugin.yaml` | Feeds every event to `on` jobs | Covered: J2 |

### 3.12 Hub and worker services, 0.5

| ID | What it is | Where | What it does | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| HUB5-001 | Hub process | 0.5: `rook/core2/hub/runtime.py` (1778 lines), `__main__.py` | Puts together keys, people, plugin library, dispatch, journal, records, vault, backups, enrollment, doors and the band transport | Covered: Topology section (hub and spoke; no line ID) |
| HUB5-002 | Hub keys | 0.5: `runtime.py` (`GRANT_DAYS = 7`, `OP_KEY_DAYS = 30`, `_daily`) | Root key (= `hub_id`) and operational key under `<data>/keys`. The root signs a `rook-hubkey` grant for the op key (7-day life, renewed daily); the op key is replaced every 30 days | Covered: R5, U9 |
| HUB5-003 | Signed hub announce | 0.5: `runtime.py _announce` (`rook-announce` v2) | The hub announces itself on the band with its grant | Not covered |
| HUB5-004 | Worker registry | 0.5: `runtime.py Known`, `_heartbeat`, `_fetch`, `workers.json`; `OFFLINE_AFTER = 90.0` | Built from announces and heartbeats. Caps stay known while a worker is offline and across restarts. Offline after 90 s. Manifests are resolved by hash from the library, else fetched with `core.manifests` and validated | Covered: H3, W5 |
| HUB5-005 | Worker key pinning | 0.5: `runtime.py` (journal kind `worker_key`) | The worker's public key is pinned from enrollment, else from the first announce that carries one. A later announce with a different key is refused | Covered: R5 |
| HUB5-006 | Hub node `rook` | 0.5: `runtime.py`, `hub/plugins.py` | Calls to worker `rook` (hub caps) go through dispatch like any call and run in-process | Covered: PL3 |
| HUB5-007 | Core events | 0.5: `builtin/core.yaml emits`; `runtime.py _emit` | worker.online/offline/updated, plugin.installed/updated/removed, hold.created/decided, session.started/ended, gate.blocked; record events `<plugin>.<record>.created/updated/archived`; worker `core.event`. Delivered to hub hooks and site listeners; not journaled | Covered: J2 |
| HUB5-008 | Hourly housekeeping | 0.5: `runtime.py _hourly` | Prunes ended sessions; walks every journal hash chain (`journal.check`) and logs CRITICAL on a break | Not covered |
| HUB5-009 | Daily housekeeping | 0.5: `runtime.py _daily` | Rotates op key and grant, prunes the journal (S-JR4), prunes records with `retain` | Out by decision: "Stream timeouts, blob garbage collection, any automatic deletion" |
| HUB5-010 | `hub.security` switch | 0.5: `runtime.py security_off()`; setting `hub.security` (default `off`); `builtin/hub/plugin.yaml settings.security` | Off: no gates and no holds in dispatch, enrollment or sign-off; the journal records `security: off` and `would_hold`; the Approvals page is hidden | Out by decision: "Approvals, security gates, elevation leases, change classes" |
| HUB5-011 | Sacred machines | 0.5: `runtime.py Config.sacred` (hub.yaml) | Changes on the named workers are hard stops | Out by decision: "Approvals, security gates, elevation leases, change classes" |
| HUB5-012 | LLM provider views | 0.5: `builtin/llm/plugin.yaml`, `caps.py llm_*` | Lists `llm.complete` providers, their models and status | Not covered (same status as the model-provider caps; may belong to the Agents plan) |
| HUB5-013 | One dispatch path, one journal entry per call | 0.5: `Dispatcher.dispatch` | Order: door, caller, session, target+cap, why, rights, gates, holds, ticket, journal; a held call gets a second entry on release or refusal | Covered: L1 |
| HUB5-014 | Sessions | 0.5: `start_session(caller, caps, project, task)`; `SESSION_SECS = 12h`; MCP `session`; `POST /session` | Narrows a caller's calls to some cap patterns and ties them to a project or task (a journal chain). Callers get a default session | Not covered |
| HUB5-015 | Why required | 0.5: dispatch step 4 (`wire.WHY_MAX`) | A call with no why, or one too long, is denied | Not covered |
| HUB5-016 | Rights intersection | 0.5: `_rights`; `rook/core2/rights.py` | Allowed only if every layer allows it: person preset+grants, agent grants, session caps, plugin `uses`, page caps, the target worker's ceiling | Out by decision: "Multi-user tenants and permissions beyond share cap lists" |
| HUB5-017 | Gates | 0.5: `rook/core2/hub/gate.py`; manifest `gate:` | Plugin gates run in parallel within a budget, in shadow or enforce mode, and can only restrict; an error counts as `flag`; the `result:` check can withhold a result | Out by decision: "Approvals, security gates, elevation leases, change classes" |
| HUB5-018 | Holds / hard stops | 0.5: `Hold`, `_hard_stops`, `release`; `hub.decide` | Calls with hard stops (destructive effects, `secret.reveal/delete`, sacred machines, gate flags) wait in memory with no expiry; release runs the exact held call | Out by decision: "Approvals, security gates, elevation leases, change classes" |
| HUB5-019 | Call tickets | 0.5: `_mint` (`rook-ticket` v2, `TICKET_SECS = 60`, nonce, `carry_hash`) | Each call to a worker carries a signed one-call ticket; re-minted once on expiry; retried once | Not covered |
| HUB5-020 | Appliance routing | 0.5: `_appliance`, `_routes`; `appliances.worked` | A call naming an appliance (or using its name as the worker) goes to its delegates: known-good first, online before offline, remembering what worked | Covered: D4, D5 |
| HUB5-021 | Secret substitution | 0.5: `secrets(...)`, `vault.resolver`, `vault.journal_args` | `{{secret:NAME}}` resolved only for the wire. The journal keeps unresolved args, with `type: secret` args redacted; values come back as `[redacted]` in errors and results | Covered: X2, L4 |
| HUB5-022 | Carry | 0.5: `carry(worker, cap, appliance)` | Sends the plugin's hub-kept settings (secrets resolved) and the appliance instance with each call, bound to the ticket | Covered: D7, CF3 |
| HUB5-023 | Contract routing | 0.5: `rook/kit/contracts.py`; `core.yaml contracts:` (notify.send, llm.complete, agent.backend, voice.*, hid.*, screen.*, media.*, ui.*, notification.*, …) | A contract is served as its own cap and routed to the chosen provider (hub setting, else the first installed) as the same caller; `route: target` contracts go to whatever provides them on the named worker | Not covered |

| HUB5-025 | Follow long calls | 0.5: `runtime.call_or_follow` | A call still running after `wait` (MCP 45 s) answers with a handle and keeps running; its reply lands in a stream to follow | Not covered |
| HUB5-026 | Elevation | 0.5: `dispatch.py` (journal kind `elevation`), `wire.LEASE_SECS = 900`; `core.elevate`, `core.lease.drop`; MCP `call elevate` | A worker asks the hub for an administrator lease mid-call | Out by decision: "Approvals, security gates, elevation leases, change classes" |
| HUB5-027 | People and presets | 0.5: `rook/core2/hub/identity.py`; `core.yaml presets` (owner, adult, child, guest with `expires: 7d`); hub.yaml `users:` | People come from config with a preset plus extra grants; a caller is a person and/or an agent | Out by decision: "Multi-user tenants and permissions beyond share cap lists" |

Also: `/register` (RFC 7591); "token clients" (`rook-<token id>` with the token as client secret, no form); approve-by-code (`/authorize/wait`, `token.approve`, CLI `oauth approve`, 10-minute codes, journaled) | Not covered |

| HUB5-036 | Tamper triggers + chain check | 0.5: `journal_store.py` triggers; `check()` hourly | UPDATE always refused, DELETE refused before the chain's checkpoint; `check` walks chains and reports rewrites | Not covered |
| HUB5-037 | Journal prune | 0.5: `journal_store.prune`; `RETENTION_DAYS = 90` | Drops the general chain one UTC day at a time 90 days after it ends (closed projects: TODO); the prune journals itself | Covered: L3 |
| HUB5-038 | Journal search | 0.5: `runtime.journal_view`; `journal.search`; `GET /journal`; MCP `journal` | Filters: cap, worker, project, kind, session, since, until, limit; filtered by the caller's rights | Covered: L2 |
| HUB5-039 | Late replies | 0.5: `runtime.py` journal kind `late_reply` | A reply that arrives after its call gave up is still journaled | Covered: L1 |
| HUB5-040 | Vault store | 0.5: `rook/core2/hub/vault/__init__.py` (`<data>/vault.db`) | Each value sealed with NaCl SecretBox under a random data key. The data key is sealed twice: under the root key (opens at start) and under the recovery key | Covered: X2, X4 |
| HUB5-041 | Secret caps owners only | 0.5: `vault/caps.py`, `vault/plugin.yaml` (`secret.list/set/delete/reveal`) | List names, descriptions and setters (never values); set; delete and reveal are hard stops | Covered: X3 |
| HUB5-042 | Secret plugin settings | 0.5: `caps.py _vault_name`; `Vault.setting` | A secret setting stores a vault name; the value travels with each call and is never written to worker disk | Covered: X5, CF3 |
| HUB5-043 | Vault recovery | 0.5: CLI `recover-vault` | Opens a `vault.db` from a dead hub with the recovery key and reseals it under this hub's root key | Covered: F3 |
| HUB5-044 | CLI secrets | 0.5: CLI `secret list \| set NAME \| delete NAME` | Value read from stdin, never argv | Not covered |

| HUB5-046 | Verify by restore | 0.5: `backup.run` | Pushes, fetches back and restores into scratch to check every backup | Covered: F3 |
| HUB5-047 | Backup targets | 0.5: setting `target`: `dir:`, `worker:<name>` (backup-store plugin), `rclone:<remote>:<path>` | Blank target = another online owner's worker running `backup-store`, not on the hub machine | Covered: F3 |

### 3.13 Client changes on castling, 0.5

| ID | What it is | Where | What it does | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| APP5-01 | Phone: Rook version switch | 0.5: `android/.../SettingsActivity.kt`, `res/layout/activity_settings.xml` ("ROOK VERSION": 0.4 / "0.5 preview"), `Castling.kt` (`PREF rook_core`, default 0.4) | Picks which worker the app runs | Not covered |
| APP5-02 | Phone: 0.5 sign-in | 0.5: `SettingsActivity.kt` ("SIGN IN TO YOUR HUB": hub address or QR, name, Sign in / Cancel sign-in); `AndroidManifest.xml` (`rook://enroll` intent filter; SettingsActivity exported, singleTop) | Enrolls by device code, or by a QR one-time code opened from the camera | Covered: A2 |
| APP5-03 | Phone: `castling_entry.py` | 0.5: `android/app/src/main/python/castling_entry.py` (179) | status / enroll / cancel / start / stop / kick / connected; everything in `<files>/rook05`; unpacks `castling_plugins.zip` | Covered: U2 |
| APP5-04 | Phone: WorkerService runs 0.5 + remote switch | 0.5: `WorkerService.kt` (+106) | Runs `castling_entry.start` when 0.5 is chosen. Polls `files/rook05/switch.json` every 15 s; a remote switch to 0.5 signs in first and goes back to 0.4 if no band link comes up within `trial_seconds` (default 300) | Not covered |
| APP5-05 | Phone: APK staging of 0.5 | 0.5: `android/stage_worker.py` (+64), `app/build.gradle` (Chaquopy `pyyaml`, `jsonschema<4.18`) | Stages `rook.core2` (minus hub, migrate), `rook.kit`, `rook.streams`, the manifest schema, and the plugins whose `platforms` list android, zipped | Covered: U1 |
| APP5-06 | Phone: CALL_PHONE permission | 0.5: `AndroidManifest.xml` | So `call.dial` can place calls | Covered: U4 |
| APP5-07 | Phone: HubDoor | 0.5: `HubDoor.kt` (63) + `HubDoorTest.kt` | `POST /call` on the 0.5 HTTP door with the person's token, worker `rook` | Not covered |

| APP5-13 | Desktop: holds in panel/notifications | 0.5: `hub05.rs waiting/attention` (`GET /holds`) | The panel's "waiting" and attention list are the person's holds | Out by decision: "Approvals, security gates, elevation leases, change classes" |
| APP5-14 | Desktop: hub pages in tray and panel | 0.5: `hub05.rs pages/page_sections` (`page.list`); `sections.rs path_for(menu_id, sections)` | Tray Dashboard menu and panel list the hub's pages by section; only listed pages open | Not covered |
| APP5-15 | Desktop: quick ask house agent | 0.5: `hub05.rs ask` (`agent.chat.send agent=house wait=true`) | Asks the house agent in the person's dock session | Agents or Workbench |
| APP5-16 | Desktop: run this computer's 0.5 worker | 0.5: `worker05.rs` (393) | Runs `python -m rook.core2.worker` as the app's child, config in `<config>/worker05/worker.yaml`; "Add this computer" = `enroll` by device code, approved by the app with `POST /enroll/approve`; plugins shell, file, notification-desktop, screen-desktop; SIGTERM stop | Covered: R2, W6 |
| APP5-17 | Desktop: voice settings from bundle | 0.5: `lib.rs` | The config bundle's voice service fills empty voice settings | Agents or Workbench |
| APP5-18 | Desktop: update check on 0.5 | 0.5: `lib.rs` (`/updates/desktop.json`) | Same update check against a 0.5 hub | Covered: U4 |
| APP5-19 | Desktop: 0.5 panel has no Work/Knowledge/Sessions lists | 0.5: `lib.rs`, `ui/panel.js` | 0.5 panel = holds, hub pages, house agent | Agents or Workbench |
| APP5-20 | Desktop: tests | 0.5: `tests/live_hub05.rs` (123), contract test | Live test against a 0.5 hub, including adding a worker | Not covered |


## 4. docs/FEATURES.md against the code

0.4's `docs/FEATURES.md` has 73 entries (45 headings, plus distinct bullets and table rows). Code was found for every entry. Six of them, among them Android voice and parts of the desktop app, were checked only by the presence of their files. Where the document and the code disagree, the code wins, and the tables above follow the code:

- Pairing: the document says "Open Tokens … Start pairing". In the code, pairing lives on Install, on Account ("Pair a device") and on Bands > Key; the Tokens page has no pairing.
- Sidebar: the document lists Feed as "(Overview, Start page)". In the code, the Feed section starts with a Feed page of its own.
- Workers view: the document says "list or grid layout". The code also has a 3D layout.
- Jobs page: the document says six tabs. The code has seven; it adds Ready-made.
- Secrets: the document says "Direct reads are possible". That is only true through MCP `rook_secret get`; the dashboard never shows a value.
- Voice: the document says every voice setting is under Settings > Voice. The code links to Settings > Plugins & services > voice; there is no Settings > Voice.
- Security roadmap: the document lists "PSK rotation tooling" as future work. The code already ships PSK rotation (Bands > Key) and per-device certificates.

The code also has things FEATURES.md does not mention. They are all in the tables above: the Feed and phone attention sorting; the Approvals, Trust map and Decisions pages; People and access grants; `/permissions`; `/show/<id>`; the Wear OS app; the `decide` plugin; change management; routines run on their own; `serves` hosting links; plugin presets and the marketplace; and `codex_history`.

## 5. How this was built

### Commits read

- 0.4: `master` at `b93ba4a5e6fbbb230a84004e57f38025c737d6e1` (3 Nov 2026), read from the `/home/bake/rook` checkout. Its working tree was clean apart from untracked worktrees.
- 0.5: `origin/castling` at `db919d7bf64e7e0fcfe149927d376d1fda147e01` (3 Nov 2026), exported with `git archive` into a scratch folder. 0.5 means `rook/core2`, `rook/kit`, `rook/plugins` and `rook/streams`, plus the castling changes to `android/` and `desktop/`. The 0.4 tree that castling still contains was read once, as 0.4. The draft manifests in `castling/manifests/` are converter output, not running code; they appear as one row (`HUB5-122`) and are not counted as caps. Draft pull requests that are not merged into `castling` were not read.
- Nothing ran against a live hub. Nothing was signed in to.
- The statuses were first set against PLAN.md as locked, then checked against amendment 1 (merged to `core` while this was being written). The amendment changes two places. The relay installer and relay settings rows now count as covered by Topology. Password login is noted on T4.

### Caps

0.4, the methods decorated with `@capability` (a script walks the AST of every file `git grep -l "@capability"` lists, outside `tests/` and `tools/`, and joins each class's `NAMESPACE` with the decorator's name):

```sh
git -C /home/bake/rook grep -l "@capability" -- '*.py'      # 450 decorated methods once tests/ and tools/ are skipped
```

0.4, the caps registered by name rather than by decorator:

```sh
git -C /home/bake/rook grep -n -E "\.register\(\s*['\"][a-z][a-z0-9_.-]*['\"]" -- 'rook/*.py' 'android/*.py'
# 19: hub node.py 4, worker admin.py 7, worker core.py 4, worker devices.py 2, android worker_runtime.py 2
```

0.4, the ESP32 dongle firmware: the caps in `CAPS_LIST` in `firmware/src/telesthete.cpp` (15). The RP2040 firmware declares no caps; its host worker serves `dongle.*`.

Some 0.4 caps exist only at runtime: `cmd.<name>` caps made with `customcap.add`, devices defined with `devkit.define`, and per-device caps. Those are counted once, as the cap that creates them.

0.5: every `plugin.yaml` under `rook/plugins` and `rook/core2`, plus `rook/core2/builtin/*.yaml`, loaded with castling's own `rook.kit.manifest.load.check` and `normalize`. These are what the hub itself serves, so the generated record caps are included. That gives 75 manifests and 407 caps.

Each cap was then given to exactly one `CAP-` row by a script that matches on file and name, and stops if any cap matches no row or two rows. The `CAP-` counts therefore add up to 484 and 407.

### MCP tools, pages, jobs, services and clients

- 0.4 MCP tools: `@mcp.tool` in `rook/band_mcp/server.py` (25) and `claude_code.py` (1). Each hub plugin's `mcp_tools(invoke)` hands over its own tools (12, of which 4 depend on settings). No cap sets `tool=True`.
- 0.5 MCP tools: the `TOOLS` table in `rook/core2/hub/doors/mcp.py`, which has 7.
- 0.4 pages: the `viewInfo` table and the `tab-*` links in `rook/web/index.html` (29 views), plus every route that serves a page outside the dashboard. In-page tabs were found by grepping the `[['key','Label']]` tables in `rook/web/*.js`.
- 0.5 pages: the `pages:` entries in the manifests (8), plus the static pages in `rook/core2/hub/doors/web/static` (3).
- 0.4 jobs: `TEMPLATES` in `rook/hub/plugins/jobs/catalog.py` (3); step kinds from `kinds.py` and `register_step_kind`; triggers from `TRIGGER_KINDS`. Background loops were found by grepping `async def …loop|tick|sweep|prune|poll` and `while True` in the hub, MCP and worker code (42 and 50 hits), then sorted by hand.
- 0.5 jobs: the `jobs:` sections in the manifests (4) and the `hooks:` sections (5 plugins with `every`, 3 with `on`).
- Services, clients, the Android and desktop apps, firmware, installers, the skill, conformance and tooling were read file by file: module docstrings, routes, manifests (Android: 7 activities, 9 services, 8 receivers, 32 permissions; Wear: 1 activity, 2 services) and `git diff --stat master origin/castling -- android desktop firmware` (34 files; no firmware changes).

This reading was split into four parts, each done by a separate reading pass: 0.4 MCP and services, 0.4 pages and jobs, 0.4 clients, and 0.5. Their tables were then merged into section 3. Where two parts listed the same thing, one row was kept: the Android, desktop and firmware rows from the services pass, the CI and installer rows, the Claude Code mod rows, the job rows that the pages-and-jobs pass covers more fully, and the cap-glue rows that duplicate a `CAP-` row. Statuses were made consistent across all parts. Every Not covered row is listed in section 1; a script checks this.

The "referenced from N files" figures in section 1 count the other files in 0.4 (tests and docs excluded) that mention a cap's name. That is a rough signal of how much depends on a cap. Short names such as `chat.` and `notify.` also match unrelated text, so the counts run high for those.

### What the code alone could not settle

- **How much each thing is used.** That needs the live hub's journal and access logs, which were not touched. "Most used" in section 1 rests on references in the code.
- **What is installed on the live hubs.** That includes which jobs are installed, which plugins are switched on, and whether a 0.5 hub has agent-made pages. Section 3 lists what exists in code, not what is switched on.
- **Telesthete's share.** Whether reply compression, Drop transfers, addressed routes and the WebSocket bridge (`WRK4-035`, `WRK4-036`, `HUB5-112`, `HUB4-035`) belong to Telesthete under the plan's Layering section was not checked against the Telesthete repo.
- **APK URL stamping (A1).** The 0.4 and 0.5 APK code was searched for signing-block stamping and none was found. The search was by name, not a full read of the APK build.
- **A1 to A3 on castling.** The phone's and desktop's castling code was read through its diff and comments, not line by line.
- **Absent in 0.5.** Heartbeat history, a redact cap, `secret.detected`, per-device polling and per-worker device opt-out were searched for and not found in `rook/core2` and `rook/kit`. A plugin that was not opened could still hold one of them.
- **Conformance vectors.** The 0.4 conformance README lists 11 vector files; 10 exist (`tiers.json` is missing).
